```
def proj(z,n):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    prev=np.clip(z,-1,1)
    s=prev.sum()
    if abs(s)<1e-12: return prev
    # bisection lambda
    lo=-2.0; hi=2.0
    # if s>0, need subtract (lambda positive)
    # S(lam)=sum(clip(z-lam,-1,1)). Find root.
    for _ in range(60):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       cur=np.clip(z-mid,-1,1).sum()
       if cur>0: lo=mid? Let's derive: S decreases as lambda increases. If S(0)=s>0, need lambda>0. lo=0? We can set lo=-2, hi=2.
       If S(mid)>0, need larger lambda -> lo=mid else hi=mid.
    lam=(lo+hi)/2
    return np.clip(z-lam,-1,1)
```

`f_vector(x,n)` uses FFT autocorr. We can cache nfft? top-level? Not closures. We can pass n. Computing nfft each call via while; cheap. Could precompute in run and pass? Functions top-level cannot closures but can accept nfft. To avoid caching global? We can define global `_FFT_LEN={}`? Might be okay? But simpler compute each call.
```
def autocorr(x,n):
    L=1
    while L < 2*n-1: L <<=1
    F=np.fft.rfft(x,L)
    return np.fft.irfft(F*np.conj(F),L).real[:n]
```
For n=700 L=2048. rfft each call. Good.

def eval_d(d):
   vals = f_aff - d*sl
   mx = vals.max() if R>0 else 0
   f0n = f[0] - (2*x0*d + d*d)/(2*n)
   if f0n > mx: mx=f0n
   if unaff > mx: mx=unaff
   return mx
```

def spectral_template(n, seed, low=0.1, high=8.0):
  rng=RandomState(seed)
  L=next fft >=2n
  k=np.arange(L)
  # use frequencies 1..M maybe M=n//2
  # power = exp(-abs(k)/s) or 1/(1+(abs(k)/s)^2)
  # random phases for real: X=0+0j; for freqs pos and neg
  # simpler use random real signal filtered? Use ifft complex Gaussian with power.
  w=np.zeros(L); freq=1..n//2; w[1:]=np.exp(-abs(freq)/scale); w[L-freq]? For real use conjugate.
  X=w * (rng.randn(L)+1j*rng.randn(L))
  x=np.fft.irfft(X,L)[:n]
  x=x - x.mean()
  m=np.max(np.abs(x)); if m>0: x/=m
  x=proj(x,n)
  # optionally clip already? normalizing max to1, proj may lower.
  return x
```

Let's implement gradient_phase with a fixed number of iterations based on time, maybe 1000 max. It uses alpha sequence internally.
```
def gradient_phase(x,n,deadline,alpha=300):
   best_c=fast_c(x,n); best_x=x
   mom=np.zeros(n); step=0.4; beta=0.3
   while time<deadline:
      F,g=smooth_params(x,n,alpha)
      if not finite: break
      if np.allclose(g,0): break
      # momentum step
      v=beta*mom + step*g
      x1=proj(x-v,n); c1=fast_c(x1,n)
      if c1 < c - 1e-15:
          x=x1; c=c1; mom=v; step=min(1.5,step*1.05); update best
      else:
          # line search no mom
          trial=step
          accepted=False
          for _ in range(10):
             x1=proj(x-trial*g,n); c1=fast_c
             if c1 < c -1e-15:
                x=x1;c=c1;mom=trial*g; step=min(1.5,trial*1.05); accepted=True; break
             trial*=0.5
          if accepted: update best
          else:
             # try very small step and maybe accept if F smooth decreases? If not, perturb?
             mom=0; step=max(0.02,step*0.6)
             # if many stalls, break to coordinate
             if step<0.02: break
   return x,best_c,best_x
```
Potential bug: if no exact descent, step reduces; eventually break. After coordinate, could restart gradient.

def coord_single_x(x,n,max_passes,deadline,gs=35):
    x=x.copy(); best=x.copy(); best_c=fast_c(x,n)
    gr=...
    for pass in range(max_passes):
       if time>deadline: break
       f=fvec(x,n); C=f.max()
       improved=False
       for p in range(n):
          if time>deadline: break
          x0=x[p]
          lo=-x0; hi=1-x0
          if hi-lo<1e-13: continue
          R=max(p,n-1-p)
          if R>0:
             L=np.zeros(R)
             if p>0:
                # r=1..min(p,R)
                L[:min(p,R)] += x[p-1::-1]
             if p<n-1:
                L[:min(n-1-p,R)] += x[p+1:n]
             aff sl = L/n? To avoid division each eval, sl=L*inv_n.
             f_aff=f[1:R+1]
             unaff = f[R+1:].max() if R+1<n else 0.0
          else: aff empty.
          f0=f[0]; x0...
          # golden search
```

def project_h(h,n):
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  target=n/2
  # bisection lam for h - lam
  lo=-1; hi=1; for 60: mid; cur=np.clip(h-mid,0,1).sum(); if cur>target: lo=mid else hi=mid
  return np.clip(h-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
```

def make_random_template(n, rng, scale=25):
   # use logarithmic frequency powers with random phases, then clip/normalize
   L=1<< (2*n-1).bit_length()
   k=np.arange(L)
   # avoid DC; power = exp(-abs(k)/scale) for k=1..L/2
   amp=np.zeros(L)
   pos=1*np.arange(1,L//2+1)
   amp[pos]=np.exp(-np.abs(pos)/scale)
   amp[L-pos]=amp[pos]  # conjugate symmetric? For ifft with complex random, no need conjugate if using complex PSD? For real ifft, need X[-k]=conj(X[k]). Use set pairs.
   # random complex: X = amp * (randn+1j randn), then make Hermitian: X = (X[0:]+X[-:])/??
   X=np.zeros(L,complex)
   for? Can't loop over L/2=1024? fine.
   rand1=rng.randn(L//2+1)
   rand2=rng.randn(L//2+1)
   posX=(rand1+1j*rand2)*amp[:L//2+1]
   X[:L//2+1]=posX
   X[L//2+1:]=np.conj(posX[1:-1][::-1]) if L even? Need careful for L even Nyquist real. Simpler: generate real Gaussian in time, filter? Or use random cos phases:
   x=np.zeros(n)
   for m in range(1, M+1):
      amp=exp(-m/scale)
      phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
      x += amp*np.cos(2π*m*np.arange(n)/n + phase)
   x=x-n.mean(); x=np.tanh(1.5*x/np.std?)? Normalize max to1; project.
```

```
def random_template_x(n,rng,scale, M=60):
  t=np.arange(n)/n
  x=np.zeros(n)
  for m in range(1, M+1):
     a=np.exp(-m/scale)
     ph=rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
     x += a*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*t + ph)
  # ensure mean zero and max bound:
  x-=x.mean()
  mabs=np.max(np.abs(x))
  if mabs>0: x/=mabs
  return proj(x,n)
```

np.correlate(h,1-h, mode='full') for sequences length n:
For shift k = index - (n-1), c[k] = ∑_{i} h[i] * (1-h)[i - k]? Let's derive formula: For a=h, v=1-h. c[k] = ∑_{n} a[n+k] v[n]. For k≥0, indices k=-(n-1)..n-1. For k=s (positive), c_s = ∑_{n=0}^{n-1-s} h[n+s] (1-h[n]). For k=-s, ∑_{n=s}^{n-1} h[n-s](1-h[n]).
So for shift s>0:
- c_s = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-s} h_{i+s}(1-h_i)
- c_{-s}=∑_{i=s}^{n-1} h_i(1-h_{i-s})
These are not symmetric and not the pair sum g. The max over full includes each directed shift separately! For indicator, c_s is number of pairs distance s where the later coordinate is 1 and earlier not? Specifically h_{i+s}(1-h_i). The max could be much larger/smaller than distance sum. Original problem statement: C5 = max_k ∫ h(x)(1-h(x+k)) dx. The integral over x such that x+k in [0,2]? It is directed for each k, not sum of both directions. They evaluate max over full correlations both signs. So objective is max over directed shifts, not symmetric pair sum. The continuous formula I derived with pair cost g is for max over both? Wait the original integral for each k: ∫_0^2 h(x)(1-h(x+k)) dx, but if x+k outside? With finite correlate, for k positive, sum over x where x+k in [0,2] h(x)(1-h(x+k)); for k negative, sum over x where x+k in [0,2] h(x)(1-h(x+k)). This is directed. The maximum over k is max of both directed shifts. It is NOT ∑ of both directions for distance. I made a big error. The previous program used np.correlate directly, so correct. Let's derive correct C in x variables.
For shift s>0:
c_s = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-s} h_{i+s}(1-h_i). Use x: h=(1+x)/2. h_{i+s}(1-h_i)=? (1+x_{i+s})/2 * (1-x_i)/2 = (1 + x_{i+s} - x_i - x_i x_{i+s})/4.

  L=nextpow2(2n-1)
  H=fftp? Use np.fft.rfft for real and product? We can use complex FFT for convenience L.

def smooth_h(h,n,alpha):
  c=full_c(h,n)
  m=c.max()
  scale=alpha*(2.0/n)*(c-m)
  e=np.exp(scale); Z=e.sum(); F=(m*2/n + np.log(Z)*(2/n)/alpha)?? Actually smooth C = dx/α log∑ exp(α dx c) = dx/α (α dx m + log Z) = dx m + dx log Z/α.
  w=e/Z
  # gradient scaled G (without dx): for each m with weight w, s=m-(n-1), add coef.
  G=np.zeros(n)
  # self m=n-1: G += w_self*(1-2h)
  G += w[n-1]*(1-2*h)
  # positive shifts m=n..2n-2 (s=1..n-1)
  # For each s, coefficient array: forward (1-h[p+s]) for p=0..n-s-1; backward -h[p-s] for p=s..n-1.
  # We can accumulate weighted shifts efficiently.
```

Smooth params h:
```
def smooth_h(h,n,alpha):
    c=full_corr(h,n)
    m=c.max()
    scale=alpha*(2.0/n)*(c-m)
    exps=np.exp(scale); Z=exps.sum(); C=m*2/n + (2/n)*np.log(Z)/alpha
    w=exps/Z
    w0=w[n-1]
    pref=np.concatenate(([0],np.cumsum(w)))
    start = (n-1) - np.arange(n)  # for p
    end = start + n - 1  # because window length n? s count n? From -p to n-1-p inclusive length n.
    A = pref[end+1] - pref[start]
    W = np.zeros(max(1,n-1))
    if n>1:
       W = w[n-1+1:2n-1] + w[n-2:-1:-1]  # r=1..n-1, positive w[n-1+r], negative w[n-1-r]
       S = forward_backward(W, h) # returns fwd+bwd
       G = A - 2*w0*h - S
    else:
       G = A - 2*w0*h # A=w0
    return C,G
```
Need ensure W indexing: w length 2n-1. For r=1..n-1: plus = w[n-1+r], minus = w[n-1-r]. So array length n-1: `w[n:2n-1]` gives indices n..2n-2 = w_{+1}..w_{n-1}. Negative: `w[n-2:0:-1]` gives w_{n-2},...,w_0 = s=-1..-(n-1). So `W = w[n:2n-1] + w[n-2:0:-1]`. Test n=3: w indices 0(-2),1(-1),2(0),3(+1),4(+2). W length2: [w3+w1, w4+w0] = r1,r2. Good.

```
def neighbor_sum(W,h):
  n=len(h); m=len(W)
  if m==0: return np.zeros(n)
  xr=h[::-1]
  cdir=fftconvolve(W,h,'full') # length n+m-1=2n-2
  bwd=np.zeros(n); bwd[1:]=cdir[:n-1]
  cmod=fftconvolve(W,xr,'full')
  fwd=np.zeros(n); fwd[:-1]=cmod[:n-1][::-1]
  return fwd+bwd
```
Check bwd: W length m=n-1. For p=1, bwd[1]=W0*h0; cdir[0]=W0*h0. p=n-1 cdir[n-2]=∑ W_r h[n-2-r]? Should be ∑_{r=1}^{n-1}W_r h[n-1-r] yes. Good.

```
def proj_h(z, n):
    z=np.asarray(z,float)
    if n==1:
      return np.array([0.5]) # target0.5? since sum=n/2=0.5, bounds yes.
    lo=-2.0; hi=2.0
    # We need S(lam)=sum clip(z-lam,0,1) = target.
    # If z already in bounds and sum target, return.
    for _ in range(45):
       mid=(lo+hi)/2
       cur=np.clip(z-mid,0,1).sum()
       if cur> n/2: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    return np.clip(z-(lo+hi)/2,0,1)
```
If z has large values, lambda beyond 2? Step small; but z could be gradient perturbation x? h in [0,1]+lr*G with G O(1), lr maybe 0.5, so z in [-0.5,1.5]. λ within [-2,2]. Good. Use 50 iterations for precision.

def random_h_template(n,rng,scale=8,M=80):

  h=np.zeros(n)

def random_template_h(n,rng,scale):
  t=np.arange(n)/n
  x=np.zeros(n)
  M=max(20, n//10)

`full_c`:
```
def full_c(h,n):
    if n==1: return np.array([h[0]*(1-h[0])])
    L=1
    m=2*n-1
    while L<m: L<<=1
    H=np.fft.rfft(h,L)
    G=np.fft.rfft(1.0-h,L)
    Z=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(H)*G,L)
    c=np.empty(2*n-1)
    c[:n-1]=Z[L-n+1:L]
    c[n-1:]=Z[0:n]
    return c
```
Double-check length: c[:n-1] leaves n-1? For n=3, c[0:2]=Z[1:3]? L-n+1=L-2, L-n+1:L = indices L-2,L-1 -> c_-2,c_-1. c[2:]=Z[0:3]. Good.

def cmax_h(h,n): return float(np.max(full_c(h,n))*2.0/n)

def proj_h(z,n):
    z=np.asarray(z,dtype=float)
    target=0.5*n
    if abs(z.sum()-target) < 1e-12 and np.all(z>=-1e-12) and np.all(z<=1+1e-12):
        z=np.clip(z,0,1); return z
    lo=-10.0; hi=10.0
    for _ in range(50):
       mid=0.5*(lo+hi)
       cur=np.clip(z-mid,0,1).sum()
       if cur>target: lo=mid
       else: hi=mid
    return np.clip(z-0.5*(lo+hi),0,1)
```

`neighbor_sum(W,h)`:
```
def neighbor_sum(W,h):
 n=len(h); m=len(W)
 out=np.zeros(n)
 if m==0: return out
 bwd=np.zeros(n)
 if m>0:
   cdir=fftconvolve(W,h,mode='full')
   bwd[1:]=cdir[:n-1]
 xr=h[::-1]
 cm=fftconvolve(W,xr,mode='full')
 fwd=np.zeros(n)
 fwd[:-1]=cm[:n-1][::-1]
 return fwd+bwd
```
Check if n=1,m=0.

`smooth_h`:
```
def smooth_h(h,n,alpha):
 dx=2.0/n
 c=full_c(h,n)
 m=c.max()
 scale=alpha*dx*(c-m)
 ex=np.exp(scale)
 Z=ex.sum()
 Csm=m*dx + dx*np.log(Z)/alpha
 w=ex/Z
 w0=w[n-1]
 pref=np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(w)))
 p=np.arange(n)
 start=n-1-p
 end=start+n-1
 A=pref[end+1]-pref[start]
 G=A - 2.0*w0*h
 if n>1:
   W=w[n:2*n-1] + w[n-2:0:-1]
   G -= neighbor_sum(W,h)
 return Csm, G
```
Need `pref[end+1]` where end max? p=0 start=n-1,end=2n-2 -> end+1=2n-1 ok. p=n-1 start=0,end=n-2 -> end+1=n-1. Good.
If n=1: w length1, w0=w[0]; start=0,end=0; A=w0; G=w0-2w0 h (if h=0.5 G=0). okay.

`gradient_phase_h`:
```
def gradient_phase_h(h,n,deadline,alpha=10000,base_step=0.25,max_iters=10000):
 h=h.copy(); best=h.copy(); best_c=cmax_h(h,n); c=best_c
 mom=np.zeros(n); step=base_step; beta=0.2; stall=0
 while time.time()<deadline and max_iters>0:
   Csm,G=smooth_h(h,n,alpha)
   ng=np.linalg.norm(G)
   if not np.isfinite(ng) or ng<1e-12: break
   v=beta*mom + step*G
   h1=proj_h(h-v,n)
   c1=cmax_h(h1,n)
   if c1 < c - 1e-14:
      h=h1; c=c1; mom=v
      if c < best_c - 1e-15: best_c=c; best=h.copy()
      step=min(1.0, step*1.05); stall=0
      continue
   # try non-momentum and half
   accepted=False
   trial=step
   for _ in range(8):
      h1=proj_h(h-trial*G,n)
      c1=cmax_h(h1,n)
      if c1 < c - 1e-14:
         h=h1; c=c1; mom=trial*G
         if c<best_c-1e-15: best_c=c; best=h.copy()
         step=min(1.0, trial*1.05)
         accepted=True; stall=0; break
      trial*=0.5
   if not accepted:
      mom=0.0
      step=max(0.02, step*0.6)
      stall+=1
      if stall>20: break
   max_iters-=1
 return best,best_c
```
Potential: If current h improved by gradient but current h not best, returning best may discard current (maybe current has lower current c? It is best if lower). If no improvement, returns original. Fine. But if no improvement but current changed? We only accept if c1 < c; current c is exact max; if accepted, current maybe best. If no, current unchanged. Good.

Could accept equal? no.

Maybe low α with smooth F can allow c increase but F decrease; exact c may not monotonic. Our line search requires exact c decrease, may stall. Could allow c1 < c + 1e-10? Might wander. Keep exact.

When step becomes small and stall, coordinate.

`coord_single_h`:
```
def _coord_eval(d, base, a, diag_idx, has_diag, h0, unaff):
    vals = base + d*a
    if has_diag:
        vals[diag_idx] = base[diag_idx] + d*(1.0 - h0) - d*d
    mx=float(vals.max())
    if unaff > mx: mx=unaff
    return mx

def coord_single_h(h,n,max_passes,deadline,gs=35):
    h=h.copy(); best=h.copy(); best_c=cmax_h(h,n); dx? Use max c no dx.
    gr=(math.sqrt(5)-1)/2
    for _pass in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       c=full_c(h,n)
       Cmax=float(c.max())
       improved=False
       for p in range(n):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          h0=h[p]
          lo=-h0; hi=1-h0
          if hi-lo<1e-13: continue
          mlo=max(0,n-1-p); mhi=min(2*n-2,n-1+p)
          Lm=mhi-mlo+1
          ms=np.arange(mlo,mhi+1)
          s=ms-(n-1)
          jf=p+s; jb=p-s
          vf=(jf>=0)&(jf<n); vb=(jb>=0)&(jb<n)
          a=np.zeros(Lm)
          if np.any(vf): a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
          if np.any(vb): a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
          dpos=np.where(ms==n-1)[0]
          has_diag = dpos.size>0
          diag_idx = int(dpos[0]) if has_diag else -1
          base=c[ms]
          unaff=0.0
          if mlo>0:
             tm=float(c[:mlo].max())
             if tm>unaff: unaff=tm
          if mhi<2*n-2:
             tm=float(c[mhi+1:].max())
             if tm>unaff: unaff=tm
          d1=hi-gr*(hi-lo); d2=lo+gr*(hi-lo)
          f1=_coord_eval(d1,base,a,diag_idx,has_diag,h0,unaff)
          f2=_coord_eval(d2,...)
          for _ in range(gs):
             if hi-lo<1e-14: break
             if f1 < f2:
                hi=d2; d2=d1; f2=f1; d1=hi-gr*(hi-lo); f1=...
             else:
                lo=d1; d1=d2; f1=f2; d2=lo+gr*(hi-lo); f2=...
          d=0.5*(lo+hi)
          fopt=_coord_eval(d,...)
          if fopt < Cmax - 1e-13:
             h[p]=h0+d
             c[ms]=base + d*a
             if has_diag: c[ms][diag_idx]? c_ms view? We did c[ms]=base+d*a, returns copy and assigns; then if has_diag need set actual index m=diag idx global c[ms[diag_idx]] = base[diag_idx]+... because diagonal override.
             if has_diag: c[int(ms[diag_idx])] = base[diag_idx]+d*(1-h0)-d*d
             Cmax=fopt
             improved=True
             if fopt*dx? Cmax unscaled. objective cmax_unscaled max; compare best_c? We stored best_c as C with dx? Need consistent. cmax_h returns scaled. Here Cmax unscaled. Let best_c_unscaled = cmax_h/h? Better in coord track `best_m = best_c * n/2`. Or compute `Cobj = fopt*2/n`. Use current cobj = Cmax*2/n. Update best if Cobj < best_c - tol. best_c scaled.
       if not improved: break
       h=proj_h(h,n)
       cobj=cmax_h(h,n)
       if cobj < best_c - 1e-15:
          best_c=cobj; best=h.copy()
       # If projection increased, best remains.
    return best,best_c
```

def polish_candidate(h,n,limit_s,alphas=(5000,20000,100000)):
    h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax_h(h,n); best=h.copy()
    if limit_s<=0: return best,best_c
    end=time.time()+limit_s
    # choose split
    for alpha in alphas:
       rem=end-time.time()
       if rem<30: break
       tgrad=min(80.0, 0.55*rem)
       if tgrad>10:
          h1,c1=gradient_phase_h(h,n,time.time()+tgrad,alpha,0.25)
          if c1<best_c: h,best_c,best=h1,c1,c? update
          # after gradient, current h may be best
       rem=end-time.time()
       if rem>20:
          tcd=min(40.0, 0.3*rem)
          h1,c1=coord_single_h(h,n,max_passes=1,deadline=time.time()+tcd,gs=35)
          if c1<best_c: h,best_c=...
       rem=end-time.time()
       if rem>25 and alpha<alphas[-1]:
          # maybe another gradient smaller
    # final coord
    if time.time()<end-10:
       h1,c1=coord_single_h(h,n,max_passes=1,deadline=end,gs=50)
       if c1<best_c: ...
    return best,best_c
```

def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time()
    deadline=t0+max(10,budget_s)-5
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    try:
       h0=np.array(initial_h_values,dtype=float).copy()
       if h0.size<2: raise ValueError
    except Exception:
       h0=np.full(700,0.5)
    n0=len(h0)
    # cap input n? If >1200, maybe downsample to 1000 to speed; but parent 700. If unknown, cap 1000.
    if n0>1000:
       h0=np.interp(np.linspace(0,1,1000),np.linspace(0,1,n0),h0)
       n0=1000
    h_best=proj_h(h0,n0)
    c_best=cmax_h(h_best,n0)
    n_best=n0
    # helper local? cannot closure? We can define top-level update? We'll just inline.
    # Phase 1 parent polish 40% time
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    if rem>90:
        lim=min(380.0, 0.5*rem)
        h1,c1=polish_candidate(h_best,n_best,lim, (5000,20000,80000))
        if c1<c_best: best...
    # Phase 2 other resolutions (up to maybe 950, 450)
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    for n_try in [950, 550]:
       if time.time()> deadline-90: break
       h_int=interp_to(h_best,n_best,n_try)
       lim=min(180.0, max(30.0, (deadline-time.time())*0.35))
       h1,c1=polish_candidate(h_int,n_try,lim,(5000,20000))
       if c1<c_best: best...
    # Phase 3 random templates/mixes
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    nrem = max(1, int((deadline-time.time())-80))
    if rem>120:
       for r in range(4):
          if time.time()>deadline-80: break
          lim=min(100.0, max(20.0, (deadline-time.time())*0.35))
          h_t=template_h(n_best,rng,4.0+3.0*r)
          # blend
          h_mix=proj_h(0.6*h_best+0.4*h_t,n_best)
          h1,c1=polish_candidate(h_mix,n_best,lim,(5000,20000))
          if c1<c_best: update
          h_t2=template_h(n_best,rng,1.0+2.0*r)
          h_mix2=proj_h(0.7*h_best+0.3*h_t2,n_best)
          if time.time()<deadline-80:
              h1,c1=polish_candidate(h_mix2,n_best,lim*0.5,(5000,20000))
              if c1<c_best: update
    # final polish
    rem=deadline-time.time()
    if rem>30:
       lim=min(rem-2, 300.0)
       h1,c1=polish_candidate(h_best,n_best,lim,(20000,80000,300000))
       if c1<c_best: update
    # final exact
    h_best=proj_h(h_best,n_best)
    c_exact=float(np.max(np.correlate(h_best,1.0-h_best,mode='full'))*2.0/n_best)
    # if c_exact slightly above returned c_best? use max? lower bound must be valid; if our c_best from full_c lower than exact due FFT bug, exact is upper? We should return exact to be safe. But if exact > c_best, and c_best claimed lower but false, harness evaluates exact and sees higher. Return exact.
    return h_best, c_exact, n_best

Template function should generate h with nonzero gradient. We need implement.

```
def template_h(n,rng,scale):
    t=np.arange(n)/float(n)
    x=np.zeros(n)
    M=max(24, min(150, n//5))
    for m in range(1,M+1):
       amp=np.exp(-m/scale)
       ph=rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0*np.pi)
       x += amp*np.cos(2.0*np.pi*m*t + ph)
    x -= x.mean()
    mx=np.max(np.abs(x))
    if mx>1e-12: x = x/mx
    else:
       x = np.sin(2*np.pi*t)  # fallback
       mx=1.0
    x = np.tanh(1.5*x) / np.tanh(1.5) # preserve bounds? tanh of x in [-1,1] maybe reduces; renormalize
    x = x / max(1e-12, np.max(np.abs(x)))
    h=0.5*(x+1.0)
    return proj_h(h,n)
```
Scale small gives high frequency; scale 8 low. Good.

   if interior.sum()>0:
      out[interior] -= diff/interior.sum()
      out=np.clip(out,0,1)
```

```
def interpolate_h(h,n_new):
    n=len(h)
    if n==n_new: return h.copy()
    old=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    new=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n_new)
    return proj_h(np.interp(new,old,h),n_new)
```

def polish_candidate(h,n,limit_s,alphas):
   h=h.copy(); best_c=cmax_h(h,n)
   if limit_s <=0: return h,best_c
   end=time.time()+limit_s
   alphas=[float(a) for a in alphas]
   for idx,alpha in enumerate(alphas):
      rem=end-time.time()
      if rem<15: break
      # first alpha use 40%, later 25%? 
      frac = 0.35 if idx==0 else 0.25

Before final, let's manually check functions syntax.

`full_c`:
```
def full_c(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,float)
    if n==1:
       return np.array([h[0]*(1-h[0])])
    L=1; need=2*n-1
    while L<need: L <<= 1
    H=np.fft.rfft(h,L)
    G=np.fft.rfft(1.0-h,L)
    Z=np.fft.irfft(np.conj(H)*G,L)
    c=np.empty(need)
    c[:n-1]=Z[n-1:0:-1]
    c[n-1]=Z[0]
    c[n:]=Z[L-1:L-n:-1]
    # clean tiny neg
    # c = np.where(c<0 & c>-1e-12,0,c) maybe not necessary.
    return c
```
Python slice `Z[L-1:L-n:-1]`: If n=2, L maybe4, L-n=2, slice 3:2:-1 -> [3]. Good. If n=3 L=4? need5 ->L=8, L-n=5, slice 7:5:-1 -> [7,6]. Good.

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.signal import fftconvolve


def proj_h(z, n):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=float)
    target = 0.5 * n
    if int(np.isclose(z.sum(), target, rtol=0.0, atol=1e-13)) and \
       float(z.min()) >= -1e-12 and float(z.max()) <= 1.0 + 1e-12:
        z = np.clip(z, 0.0, 1.0)
        return z
    lo, hi = -10.0, 10.0
    for _ in range(55):
        mid = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
        cur = float(np.clip(z - mid, 0.0, 1.0).sum())
        if cur > target:
            lo = mid
        else:
            hi = mid
    return np.clip(z - 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.0, 1.0)


def full_c(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([h[0] * (1.0 - h[0])])
    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < need:
        L <<= 1
    H = np.fft.rfft(h, L)
    G = np.fft.rfft(1.0 - h, L)
    Z = np.fft.irfft(np.conj(H) * G, L)
    c = np.empty(need)
    c[:n - 1] = Z[n - 1:0:-1]
    c[n - 1] = Z[0]
    c[n:] = Z[L - 1:L - n:-1]
    return c


def cmax_h(h, n):
    return float(np.max(full_c(h, n)) * (2.0 / n))


def interp_h(h, n_new):
    n = len(h)
    if n == n_new:
        return h.copy()
    old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_new)
    return proj_h(np.interp(new, old, h), n_new)


def neighbor_sum(W, h):
    n = len(h)
    m = len(W)
    res = np.zeros(n)
    if m == 0:
        return res
    cdir = fftconvolve(W, h, mode='full')
    bwd = np.zeros(n)
    bwd[1:] = cdir[:n - 1]
    xr = h[::-1]
    cmod = fftconvolve(W, xr, mode='full')
    fwd = np.zeros(n)
    fwd[:-1] = cmod[:n - 1][::-1]
    return fwd + bwd


def smooth_h(h, n, alpha):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = full_c(h, n)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    scale = alpha * dx * (c - m)
    ex = np.exp(scale)
    Z = float(np.sum(ex))
    Csm = m * dx + dx * math.log(max(Z, 1e-300)) / alpha
    w = ex / Z
    w0 = w[n - 1]
    pref = np.concatenate(([0.0], np.cumsum(w)))
    p = np.arange(n)
    start = n - 1 - p
    end = start + n - 1
    A = pref[end + 1] - pref[start]
    G = A - 2.0 * w0 * h
    if n > 1:
        W = w[n:2 * n - 1] + w[n - 2::-1]
        G = G - neighbor_sum(W, h)
    return Csm, G


def gradient_phase_h(h, n, deadline, alpha=20000.0, base_step=0.25):
    h = h.copy()
    best_h = h.copy()
    c = cmax_h(h, n)
    best_c = c
    mom = np.zeros(n)
    step = base_step
    beta = 0.18
    stall = 0
    while time.time() < deadline:
        Csm, G = smooth_h(h, n, alpha)
        ng = float(np.linalg.norm(G))
        if not math.isfinite(ng) or ng < 1e-13:
            break
        v = beta * mom + step * G
        h1 = proj_h(h - v, n)
        c1 = cmax_h(h1, n)
        if c1 < c - 1e-14:
            h, c = h1, c1
            mom = v
            if c < best_c - 1e-15:
                best_c, best_h = c, h.copy()
            step = min(1.0, step * 1.06)
            stall = 0
            continue
        trial = step
        ok = False
        for _ in range(9):
            h1 = proj_h(h - trial * G, n)
            c1 = cmax_h(h1, n)
            if c1 < c - 1e-14:
                h, c = h1, c1
                mom = trial * G
                if c < best_c - 1e-15:
                    best_c, best_h = c, h.copy()
                step = min(1.0, trial * 1.06)
                ok = True
                stall = 0
                break
            trial *= 0.5
        if not ok:
            mom = np.zeros(n)
            step = max(0.015, step * 0.6)
            stall += 1
            if stall > 25:
                break
    return best_h, best_c


def _coord_eval(d, base, a, diag_idx, has_diag, h0, unaff):
    vals = base + d * a
    if has_diag:
        vals[diag_idx] = base[diag_idx] + d * (1.0 - h0) - d * d
    mx = float(np.max(vals))
    if unaff > mx:
        mx = unaff
    return mx


def coord_single_h(h, n, max_passes, deadline, gs=35):
    h = h.copy()
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_m = float(np.max(full_c(h, n)))
    dx = 2.0 / n
    gr = (math.sqrt(5.0) - 1.0) / 2.0
    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        c = full_c(h, n)
        curr_m = float(np.max(c))
        improved = False
        for p in range(n):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            h0 = h[p]
            lo = -h0
            hi = 1.0 - h0
            if hi - lo < 1e-13:
                continue
            mlo = max(0, n - 1 - p)
            mhi = min(2 * n - 2, n - 1 + p)
            ms = np.arange(mlo, mhi + 1)
            s = ms - (n - 1)
            jf = p + s
            jb = p - s
            vf = (jf >= 0) & (jf < n)
            vb = (jb >= 0) & (jb < n)
            a = np.zeros(len(ms))
            if np.any(vf):
                a[vf] += 1.0 - h[jf[vf]]
            if np.any(vb):
                a[vb] -= h[jb[vb]]
            dpos = np.where(ms == n - 1)[0]
            has_diag = dpos.size > 0
            diag_idx = int(dpos[0]) if has_diag else -1
            base = c[ms]
            unaff = 0.0
            if mlo > 0:
                unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[:mlo])))
            if mhi < 2 * n - 2:
                unaff = max(unaff, float(np.max(c[mhi + 1:])))
            d1 = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
            d2 = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
            f1 = _coord_eval(d1, base, a, diag_idx, has_diag, h0, unaff)
            f2 = _coord_eval(d2, base, a, diag_idx, has_diag, h0, unaff)
            for _it in range(gs):
                if hi - lo < 1e-14:
                    break
                if f1 < f2:
                    hi = d2
                    d2 = d1
                    f2 = f1
                    d1 = hi - gr * (hi - lo)
                    f1 = _coord_eval(d1, base, a, diag_idx, has_diag, h0, unaff)
                else:
                    lo = d1
                    d1 = d2
                    f1 = f2
                    d2 = lo + gr * (hi - lo)
                    f2 = _coord_eval(d2, base, a, diag_idx, has_diag, h0, unaff)
            dopt = 0.5 * (lo + hi)
            fopt = _coord_eval(dopt, base, a, diag_idx, has_diag, h0, unaff)
            if fopt < curr_m - 1e-13:
                h[p] = h0 + dopt
                for idx, gi in enumerate(ms):
                    c[int(gi)] = base[idx] + dopt * a[idx]
                if has_diag:
                    c[int(ms[diag_idx])] = base[diag_idx] + dopt * (1.0 - h0) - dopt * dopt
                curr_m = fopt
                improved = True
        if not improved:
            h = proj_h(h, n)
            m2 = float(np.max(full_c(h, n)))
            if m2 < best_m - 1e-15:
                best_m, best_h = m2, h.copy()
            break
        h = proj_h(h, n)
        m2 = float(np.max(full_c(h, n)))
        if m2 < best_m - 1e-15:
            best_m, best_h = m2, h.copy()
        else:
            h = best_h.copy()
            break
    return best_h, best_m * dx


def template_h(n, rng, scale):
    t = np.arange(n) / float(max(1, n))
    x = np.zeros(n)
    M = max(24, min(160, n // 5))
    for m in range(1, M + 1):
        amp = math.exp(-float(m) / float(scale))
        ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * math.pi)
        x += amp * np.cos(2.0 * math.pi * m * t + ph)
    x -= x.mean()
    mx = float(np.max(np.abs(x)))
    if mx < 1e-12:
        x = np.sin(2.0 * math.pi * t)
        mx = 1.0
    x = x / max(1e-12, mx)
    y = np.tanh(1.6 * x)
    my = float(np.max(np.abs(y)))
    if my > 1e-12:
        x = y / my
    return proj_h(0.5 * (x + 1.0), n)


def polish_candidate(h, n, limit_s, alphas=(5000.0, 20000.0, 80000.0)):
    h = proj_h(h, n)
    best_c = cmax_h(h, n)
    end = time.time() + max(0.0, float(limit_s))
    if end <= time.time():
        return h, best_c
    alphas = [float(a) for a in alphas]
    if not alphas:
        alphas = [20000.0]
    for idx, alpha in enumerate(alphas):
        if time.time() > end - 25.0:
            break
        rem = end - time.time()
        tg = min(100.0, 0.38 * rem)
        if tg > 8.0:
            h1, c1 = gradient_phase_h(h, n, time.time() + tg, alpha, 0.25)
            h = h1
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_c = c1
        rem = end - time.time()
        if rem > 18.0:
            tcd = min(52.0, 0.22 * rem)
            if tcd > 6.0:
                h1, c1 = coord_single_h(h, n, 1, time.time() + tcd, 30)
                h = h1
                if c1 < best_c:
                    best_c = c1
    rem = end - time.time()
    if rem > 30.0:
        h1, c1 = gradient_phase_h(h, n, time.time() + 0.55 * rem, alphas[-1] * 1.25, 0.16)
        h = h1
        if c1 < best_c:
            best_c = c1
        if time.time() < end - 12.0:
            h1, c1 = coord_single_h(h, n, 2, end, 45)
            h = h1
            if c1 < best_c:
                best_c = c1
    h = proj_h(h, n)
    ch = cmax_h(h, n)
    if ch < best_c:
        best_c = ch
    return h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    deadline = t0 + max(15.0, float(budget_s)) - 5.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).copy()
        if h0.size < 2:
            raise ValueError("bad")
    except Exception:
        h0 = np.full(700, 0.5)
    n0 = len(h0)
    if n0 > 1000:
        old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n0)
        new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, 1000)
        h0 = np.interp(new, old, h0)
        n0 = 1000

    h_best = proj_h(h0, n0)
    c_best = cmax_h(h_best, n0)
    n_best = n0

    if c_best > 0.70 and time.time() < deadline - 100.0:
        h_alt = template_h(n_best, rng, 8.0)
        c_alt = cmax_h(h_alt, n_best)
        if c_alt < c_best:
            h_best, c_best = h_alt, c_alt

    if time.time() < deadline - 120.0:
        lim = min(430.0, 0.55 * (deadline - time.time()))
        h1, c1 = polish_candidate(h_best, n_best, lim, (5000.0, 20000.0, 80000.0))
        if c1 < c_best:
            h_best, c_best, n_best = h1, c1, n_best

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 160.0:
        for n_try in (950, 550):
            if time.time() > deadline - 90.0:
                break
            if n_try == n_best and rem < 200.0:
                continue
            h_int = interp_h(h_best, n_try)
            lim = min(190.0, 0.34 * (deadline - time.time()))
            if lim < 35.0:
                continue
            h1, c1 = polish_candidate(h_int, n_try, lim, (5000.0, 20000.0))
            if c1 < c_best:
                h_best, c_best, n_best = h1, c1, n_try

    if time.time() < deadline - 130.0:
        for rp in range(4):
            if time.time() > deadline - 90.0:
                break
            lim = min(115.0, max(30.0, 0.32 * (deadline - time.time())))
            h_t = template_h(n_best, rng, 3.5 + 2.5 * rp)
            h_mix = proj_h(0.62 * h_best + 0.38 * h_t, n_best)
            h1, c1 = polish_candidate(h_mix, n_best, lim, (5000.0, 20000.0))
            if c1 < c_best:
                h_best, c_best = h1, c1
            if time.time() < deadline - 90.0:
                h_t2 = template_h(n_best, rng, 1.2 + 1.8 * rp)
                h_mix2 = proj_h(0.75 * h_best + 0.25 * h_t2, n_best)
                h1, c1 = polish_candidate(h_mix2, n_best, 0.55 * lim, (5000.0, 20000.0))
                if c1 < c_best:
                    h_best, c_best = h1, c1

    rem = deadline - time.time()
    if rem > 35.0:
        lim = min(rem - 2.0, 330.0)
        h1, c1 = polish_candidate(h_best, n_best, lim, (20000.0, 80000.0, 300000.0))
        if c1 < c_best:
            h_best, c_best, n_best = h1, c1, n_best

    h_best = proj_h(h_best, n_best)
    c_exact = float(np.max(np.correlate(h_best, 1.0 - h_best, mode='full')) * (2.0 / n_best))
    return h_best, c_exact, n_best
```
